if we enable contacts, would it make the extension guest list slow to load?

> AGENT

Short answer: **yes, if we wire it the naive way — but it doesn't have to be, and the engine already has the pieces to avoid it.** Let me ground this in what the resolver actually does.

The cost in `calendar-contacts.ts` has three parts:

1. **A mandatory warmup tax.** Google's People search APIs return nothing until you've made a prior empty-query "priming" call — and there are *two* (`searchContacts` + `otherContacts.search`, lines 33-35). That's 2 extra round-trips paid **once per worker process**. It's cached in a `WeakSet`, so steady-state is free — but every serverless cold start re-pays it.

2. **Up to 2 sequential calls per guest.** Per email it tries `searchContacts`, and only if that misses, `otherContacts.search` (lines 38-40) — sequential `await ?? await`. So a guest who *isn't* a saved contact (the common visitor!) costs the *full* 2 calls before falling back.

3. **It runs server-side inside the draft fetch.** This resolution sits in the engine's draft path, which the side panel **awaits before painting the guest list**. So every added millisecond is directly in front of the list.

Worst case — a 5-guest event on a cold worker, nobody cached: ~2 warmup calls + up to 5×2 sequential People calls. At ~150-350ms each that's **easily 1-3 seconds** tacked onto the list load. That's real, and on patchy office Wi-Fi it'd be worse. So your instinct is correct.

**But none of that is forced.** The mitigations, roughly in order of impact:

- **Don't block the list on it.** Return the draft immediately with the email-fallback names, then resolve real names in the background and push them in. The extension already does **realtime guest updates** (per the extension CLAUDE.md), so the list paints instantly and names "fill in" a beat later. This is the right answer under our four constraints — fast first paint, names are cosmetic.
- **Only look up guests with no real name.** Skip anyone Calendar already named — most lookups vanish.
- **Parallelize** the remaining lookups (`Promise.all`) so latency is the *slowest* call, not the *sum*.
- **Persist the cache.** Today it's a process-level `Map` (line 22) that dies on cold start. Caching `email → name` in Postgres makes the second view of any guest instant and removes most warmups from the critical path.

So: enabling contacts naively *would* make the list slow; enabling it **as a background enrichment over an instantly-painted list** would not — the user sees the list immediately and the name upgrades silently. That progressive approach is also the only one that fits "fast and light on cheap hardware / tolerate poor connectivity."

This actually strengthens the earlier recommendation: if we do Option B at all, it should be background/streamed, never inline-blocking. Want me to confirm the realtime push channel the draft path uses (so we know the "fill in later" path is actually wired), or is this enough to decide?

> DEVELOPER

but if we return the draft immediately and the user starts editing the list and then we update the list later, would not the user get surprised?

> AGENT

Yes — and that surprise is a real bug in the *current* reconciliation code, not just a theoretical worry. Look at `RosterRow.tsx:36-37`:

```js
useEffect(() => setName(guest.name), [guest.name]);
useEffect(() => setPhone(guest.phone ?? ''), [guest.phone]);
```

That effect **blindly resets the local input whenever the server's `guest.name` changes.** So if the host has the drawer open typing "Junaid A…" and a background resolution lands "Junaid Ahmad" (or even just flips the fallback), their half-typed value gets clobbered mid-keystroke. That's exactly the surprise you're picturing, and it would also silently discard their edit.

So background resolution is only safe if we change two things about *how* the update is applied. The principle is **fill, never overwrite**:

1. **Only apply a resolved name to a field the user hasn't touched** — i.e. where the value is still the system email-fallback. The moment the host types anything (the field is "dirty"), or the name is already a real one, the resolver leaves it alone. A late name lands only in empty/default slots, so it can never overwrite intent.

2. **Never reconcile a focused or dirty input.** Guard that `useEffect` so it doesn't reset local state while the field is being edited. Resolution becomes a one-shot enrichment, not a continuous stream that fights the keyboard.

With those two rules, the worst a late resolution can do is *upgrade a name the host was about to fix anyway* — and even then, only before they've started.

Two things make the residual surprise small in practice:

- **The drawer is collapsed by default** (`RosterRow.tsx:33`). Editing is a deliberate open. Resolution almost always lands during the "reading the list" phase, before anyone opens a card to type.
- **The email is always shown as the secondary line** (`RosterRow.tsx:86`), so identity is never wrong or hidden while the name catches up — the name just gets *nicer*, it never changes *who* the row is.

If you want to shrink the race window even further, there's a clean middle path: **wait a short beat before first paint** — give the resolver, say, ~300-400ms to come back from a warm/cached worker, and if it does, paint *with* the real names from the start (no later update at all). Only when resolution is genuinely slow (cold start, patchy Wi-Fi) do we fall back to paint-now-fill-later. That makes the "names pop in afterward" path the rare exception rather than the norm, so most of the time there's nothing to be surprised by.

So: the surprise is avoidable, but only if we (a) make the merge non-destructive and (b) stop the existing effect from clobbering live edits. If we ever do Option B, those two are part of it — not optional polish.

Want me to capture this whole thread (Calendar omits external names → engine resolver exists but unused → background+fill-don't-overwrite is the safe shape) in a memory note so we don't relitigate it, and then move on to the dynamic label (#2) and inline button (#3)?

> DEVELOPER

okay then, implement this and all the other fixes and improvements we discussed. first tell me the plan and order

> AGENT

Good — before I lock the plan, one thing affects the label logic (#2): I need to see exactly how the engine populates `guest.name` in the draft (whether it's empty when there's no real name, or always backfilled from the email). Let me check the draft route, then give you the plan and order.

```
256	/**
257	 * Build the curated roster from raw attendees: drop the host + blanks, dedup
258	 * case-insensitively, derive a name when absent, pre-fill phone via `phoneLookup`,
259	 * and set each guest's DEFAULT toggle via `includeDecider` (so same-workspace /
260	 * same-location colleagues come pre-toggled-OFF — same rule as every other channel).
261	 * Pure (DB + classification injected) → unit-testable.
262	 */
263	export async function buildRoster(
264	  attendees: { email: string; name?: string }[],
265	  hostEmail: string | undefined,
266	  phoneLookup: PhoneLookup,
267	  includeDecider: IncludeDecider = () => true,
268	): Promise<DraftGuest[]> {
269	  const host = hostEmail?.toLowerCase();
270	  const seen = new Set<string>();

275	    seen.add(email);

283	      // different-office guests are always default-on). A display hint for the card.
284	      internal: !include,
285	      status: 'pending',
286	    });
287	  }
288	  return roster;
289	}
```

> AGENT

Confirmed the linchpin: `visit-drafts.ts:279` **always** backfills `name` with `nameFromEmail(email)` when there's no real display name. So `guest.name` is *never* empty — the extension currently can't tell a real name ("Junaid Ahmad") from the email-derived fallback ("J7654894110"). That one fact shapes the order: both the dynamic label and the contacts feature need the engine to *signal* whether a name is real. So I'll add a small flag first and build everything on it.

## Plan & order

**Foundation**
1. **Add `nameIsFallback` flag to `DraftGuest`** (engine `visit-drafts.ts` + extension `lib/types.ts`). Set in `buildRoster` (`!attendee.name?.trim()`). Additive, no scope change. Everything below keys off this.

**Phase 1 — extension-only, ship immediately (no new scopes)**
2. **RosterRow: non-destructive reconciliation guard.** Fix the `useEffect` at `RosterRow.tsx:36-37` so a server/background update only fills a field the host *hasn't* edited — never clobbers a live edit. (Prereq for background name updates *and* a standalone correctness fix.)
3. **RosterRow: four-way dynamic drawer label** — "Add phone and name" / "Add phone" / "Add name" / "Edit details", driven by `nameIsFallback` + phone presence.
4. **calendar.content.ts: inline "Manage Visitors" next to Save / More options.** Promote the inline-before-Save anchor to primary for the editor; keep the full-width row as fallback. Confirm the floating FAB last-resort is intact (Q4 — it is).

**Phase 2 — contacts name + photo (cross-repo; re-adds the contacts scope)**
5. **Engine: turn on the existing `resolveNameFromContacts`** — add `photos` to the readMask, parallelize, resolve **only** guests where `nameIsFallback`, run it as a **background enrichment** over an already-returned draft, persist the cache, and push the upgraded name/photo over the realtime channel. (I'll read the draft route + realtime path first.)
6. **Extension: add `contacts.readonly` + `contacts.other.readonly` scopes** (this forces a one-time re-consent), render the photo in the avatar with monogram fallback, and let the resolved name/photo flow in through the non-destructive merge from step 2. Bump `package.json` version.

**Heads-up on Phase 2:** step 6 re-adds the contacts scope, which **reverses your earlier "drop contacts scope" decision** and requires an engine deploy + each user reconnecting before names/photos actually resolve. It's a *sensitive* scope, not restricted — so still no CASA, no Gmail/Drive line crossed. Until deployed+reconnected, everything degrades gracefully to the fallback names. Phase 1 is independent and ships without any of that.

I'll go in this order. Starting with the foundation flag now.

```json
  "new_string": "  // Reconcile server/background updates WITHOUT clobbering a live edit: adopt the\n  // new server value only when the local field still equals the last server value\n  // we saw (the host hasn't typed over it). So a background name/photo resolve fills\n  // an untouched field, but a half-typed correction is never lost. (See the contacts\n  // name-resolution flow — the name can change after first paint.)\n  const lastServerName = useRef(guest.name);\n  useEffect(() => {\n    setName((local) => (local === lastServerName.current ? guest.name : local));\n    lastServerName.current = guest.name;\n  }, [guest.name]);\n\n  const lastServerPhone = useRef(guest.phone ?? '');\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const next = guest.phone ?? '';\n    setPhone((local) => (local === lastServerPhone.current ? next : local));\n    lastServerPhone.current = next;\n  }, [guest.phone]);"
}
```

```json
  "new_string": "    if (/\\/eventedit/.test(location.pathname)) {\n      // Primary: an inline button right before the native \"Save\" in the editor's\n      // action bar, so it sits next to Save / More options and reads as a native\n      // inline action — what the host wants on a new/unsaved event.\n      const save = findButtonByText('save');\n      const saveParent = save?.parentElement;\n      if (save && saveParent) {\n        return { insert: (el) => saveParent.insertBefore(el, save), inset: 0, inline: true };\n      }\n      // Fallback: our own full-width row just BELOW the Availability/Visibility info\n      // section (else above Description) when the action bar can't be found.\n      const avail = findAvailabilityInfo();\n      const afterAvail = avail ? rowsListFor(avail) : null;\n      if (afterAvail) {\n        const { list, row } = afterAvail;\n        return { insert: (el) => list.insertBefore(el, row.nextSibling), inset: rowInset(row, list) };\n      }\n      const desc = findEditDescription();\n      const aboveDesc = desc ? rowsListFor(desc) : null;\n      if (aboveDesc) {\n        const { list, row } = aboveDesc;\n        return { insert: (el) => list.insertBefore(el, row), inset: rowInset(row, list) };\n      }\n      return null;\n    }"
```

```
725	      const iCalUid = String(body.iCalUid ?? '').trim();
726	      if (!iCalUid) { json(res, 422, { error: 'iCalUid required' }); return true; }
727	      const attendees = Array.isArray(body.attendees)
728	        ? (body.attendees as { email: string; name?: string }[]).filter((a) => a && typeof a.email === 'string')
729	        : [];
730	      // Card-open path → trusted, complete attendee list → reconcile removals
731	      // (drop + cancel guests removed from the event). Plan §8B.
732	      const draft = await getOrCreateDraft({
733	        iCalUid,
734	        providerEventId: body.providerEventId ? String(body.providerEventId) : undefined,
735	        title: body.title ? String(body.title) : undefined,
736	        hostEmail: identity.email,
737	        attendees,
738	        eventStart: body.start ? String(body.start) : undefined,
739	        eventEnd: body.end ? String(body.end) : undefined,
740	        location: body.location ? String(body.location) : undefined,
741	      }, { reconcileRemovals: true });
742	
743	      // Gap A — link this event for background lifecycle (move/delete via the watch),
744	      // but only when X is the event ORGANIZER (don't attach lifecycle to meetings
745	      // X was merely invited to) and a pass actually exists for it. Keyed on the
746	      // real iCalUid so the watch's ingest finds it. Reuses the D8 link writer.
747	      const organizerEmail = body.organizerEmail ? String(body.organizerEmail).toLowerCase() : '';
748	      const isOrganizer = !!organizerEmail && organizerEmail === identity.email.toLowerCase();
749	      const sentIds = draft.roster.filter((g) => g.status === 'sent' && g.invitationId).map((g) => g.invitationId as string);
750	      if (isOrganizer && sentIds.length > 0) {
751	        await recordInvitationCalendarLink({
752	          iCalUid,
753	          providerEventId: body.providerEventId ? String(body.providerEventId) : undefined,
754	          organizerEmail: identity.email,
755	          invitationIds: sentIds,
756	          source: 'addon-link',
757	          eventStart: draft.eventStart ?? null,
758	          eventEnd: draft.eventEnd ?? null,
759	        }).catch((e) => console.warn('[Addon] event link failed:', e));
760	      }

762	      // Gap C — tell the card whether background auto-sync (move/delete) is active.
763	      // Connected → seamless. Not connected → the card offers a one-tap Connect.

765	      const calendarSync = {
766	        connected,
767	        canConnect: isCalendarOAuthConfigured(),

785	    // POST /addon/status — read-only batch check: which of these events already

789	    // /draft this is side-effect-free — no lazy draft creation, no link
790	    // recording — so it is cheap and safe to poll. Capped to bound the work.
791	    if (pathname === '/api/visitor/calendar/addon/status' && method === 'POST') {
792	      const body = await readBodyJson(req);
793	      const iCalUids = (Array.isArray(body.iCalUids) ? body.iCalUids : [])
794	        .map((v: unknown) => String(v ?? '').trim())
795	        .filter(Boolean)
796	        .slice(0, 100);
797	      const active: string[] = [];
798	      for (const uid of iCalUids) {
799	        const draft = await getDraft(uid);
800	        if (draft?.roster.some((g) => g.status === 'sent')) active.push(uid);
801	      }
802	      json(res, 200, { active });
803	      return true;
804	    }
805	
806	    // PATCH /addon/draft — persist a host edit (include toggle / name / phone / templates / location).
807	    if (pathname === '/api/visitor/calendar/addon/draft' && method === 'PATCH') {
808	      const body = await readBodyJson(req);
809	      const iCalUid = String(body.iCalUid ?? '').trim();
810	      if (!iCalUid) { json(res, 422, { error: 'iCalUid required' }); return true; }
811	      const updated = await patchDraft(iCalUid, body.patch as DraftPatch | undefined);
```

```
17	/** The pending event id, read reactively from storage.session. */
18	export function useActiveEid(): string | null {
19	  const [eid, setEid] = useState<string | null>(null);
20	  useEffect(() => {
21	    chrome.storage.session
22	      .get(ACTIVE_EID_KEY)
23	      .then((r) => setEid((r[ACTIVE_EID_KEY] as string) ?? null));
24	    const listener = (
25	      changes: { [k: string]: chrome.storage.StorageChange },
26	      area: string,
27	    ) => {
28	      if (area === 'session' && changes[ACTIVE_EID_KEY]) {
29	        setEid((changes[ACTIVE_EID_KEY].newValue as string) ?? null);
30	      }
31	    };
32	    chrome.storage.onChanged.addListener(listener);
33	    return () => chrome.storage.onChanged.removeListener(listener);
34	  }, []);
35	  return eid;
36	}
37

45	    const listener = (
46	      changes: { [k: string]: chrome.storage.StorageChange },
47	      area: string,

59	export function useAuthStatus() {

101	  return useQuery({

103	    queryFn: () => rpc({ type: 'DRAFT_LOAD', event: event! }),
104	    enabled: !!event?.iCalUid,
105	  });
106	}

108	/** Merge a returned VisitDraft into the cached DraftResponse (keep extras). */
109	function mergeDraft(
110	  prev: DraftResponse | undefined,
111	  next: VisitDraft,
112	): DraftResponse | undefined {
113	  if (!prev) return prev;
114	  return { ...prev, ...next };
115	}
116	
117	/** Optimistic patch (toggle / name / phone / template / location). */
118	export function usePatchDraft(iCalUid: string | undefined) {
119	  const qc = useQueryClient();
120	  const key = ['draft', iCalUid];
121	  return useMutation({
122	    mutationFn: (patch: DraftPatch) =>
123	      rpc({ type: 'DRAFT_PATCH', iCalUid: iCalUid!, patch }),
124	    onMutate: async (patch) => {
125	      await qc.cancelQueries({ queryKey: key });
126	      const prev = qc.getQueryData<DraftResponse>(key);
127	      if (prev) qc.setQueryData<DraftResponse>(key, applyPatch(prev, patch));
128	      return { prev };
129	    },
130	    onError: (_e, _patch, ctx) => {
131	      if (ctx?.prev) qc.setQueryData(key, ctx.prev);
132	    },
133	    onSuccess: (updated) => {
134	      qc.setQueryData<DraftResponse>(key, (prev) => mergeDraft(prev, updated));
135	    },
136	  });
137	}
138	
139	/** Apply a DraftPatch to a cached DraftResponse for the optimistic view. */
140	function applyPatch(draft: DraftResponse, patch: DraftPatch): DraftResponse {
141	  const roster = draft.roster.map((g) => {
142	    const edit = patch.guests?.find(
143	      (e) => e.email.toLowerCase() === g.email.toLowerCase(),
144	    );
145	    return edit ? { ...g, ...stripEmail(edit) } : g;
146	  });
147	  return {
148	    ...draft,
149	    roster,
150	    location: patch.location ?? draft.location,
151	  };
152	}
153	
154	function stripEmail<T extends { email: string }>(e: T): Omit<T, 'email'> {
155	  const { email: _email, ...rest } = e;
156	  return rest;
157	}
158	
159	export function useSend(iCalUid: string | undefined, start?: string, end?: string) {
160	  const qc = useQueryClient();
161	  return useMutation({
162	    mutationFn: () => rpc({ type: 'SEND', iCalUid: iCalUid!, start, end }),
163	    onSuccess: (res) => {
164	      qc.setQueryData<DraftResponse>(['draft', iCalUid], (prev) =>
165	        mergeDraft(prev, res.draft),
166	      );
167	    },
168	  });
169	}
170	
171	export function usePreview(iCalUid: string | undefined) {
172	  return useMutation({
173	    mutationFn: (visitorEmail: string) =>
174	      rpc({ type: 'PREVIEW', iCalUid: iCalUid!, visitorEmail }),
175	  });
176	}
177	
178	/** Upcoming visitor events (the synced magic-address set) for the picker. */
179	export function useVisitorEvents(enabled: boolean) {
180	  return useQuery({
181	    queryKey: ['visitorEvents'],
182	    queryFn: () => rpc({ type: 'LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS' }),
183	    enabled,
184	    staleTime: 30_000,
185	  });
186	}
```

```
1	/**
2	 * Per-user Google Calendar OAuth — connect flow + connection store.
3	 *
4	 * Canonical design: Planning-docs/2026-06-12_calendar_to_visitor_invite_research.md §8.
5	 *
6	 * A host connects once (one tap from the Slack App Home / first-DM button). We store
7	 * a long-lived refresh token in `calendar_connections`, then `calendar-sync.ts` polls
8	 * their calendar and feeds events into `ingestCalendarEvent` as source 'oauth'.
9	 *
10	 * Reuses the admin-SSO OAuth client credentials (GOOGLE_OAUTH_*) — fine for
11	 * internal-first (each flow requests only its own scopes; that client already
12	 * carried calendar-read). Scope is a single `calendar.events` (read+write):
13	 * read powers the lifecycle watch (move/delete of tracked events); write powers
14	 * creating the event on a Slack-self-host's calendar (D8). calendar.events is
15	 * "sensitive", NOT restricted → still no CASA. Never add Gmail/Drive.
16	 *
17	 * For a non-expiring refresh token the OAuth app must be "In production" (Testing =
18	 * 7-day expiry). See research doc §8.
19	 */
20	
21	import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto';
22	import { google } from 'googleapis';
23	import { getDb } from '../../lib/database';
24	import { googleOauth as googleOauthConfig, auxilioExtensionOauth } from '../../lib/config';
25	
26	const STATE_TTL_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
27	const DEFAULT_TENANT_ID = 'appointy';
28	
29	/**
30	 * Single scope: calendar.events (READ+WRITE). Read = the lifecycle watch; write =
31	 * creating the event on a Slack-self-host's calendar (D8, so it's trackable + the
32	 * guest is invited natively). "Sensitive", NOT restricted → no CASA. Contacts were
33	 * dropped: name resolution lives in the add-on (its own People API) + the invite
34	 * forms; the poll no longer creates invites, so backend contacts is dead. Never
35	 * add Gmail/Drive here.
36	 */
37	const CALENDAR_SCOPES = [
38	  'https://www.googleapis.com/auth/calendar.events',
39	];
40	
41	/**
42	 * Bump whenever CALENDAR_SCOPES changes. NOTE: this does NOT force a reconnect —
43	 * `listScopeStaleConnections` only *flags* older-version connections (their token
44	 * still covers calendar-read, so the watch keeps working). We prompt the write
45	 * upgrade lazily (only when a Slack host needs to create an event). Connections at
46	 * this version are write-capable (`calendar.events`); older ones are read-only.
47	 */
48	export const CURRENT_SCOPE_VERSION = 'v3-events-rw';
49	
50	/**
51	 * Scope marker for connections created by the Chrome extension sign-in. Readonly
52	 * (`calendar.events.readonly`) — enough for the poll + watch, never write-capable.
53	 * Kept distinct so isWriteCapableConnection stays false and the Slack reconnect
54	 * nudge (listScopeStaleConnections) skips these.
55	 */
56	export const EXTENSION_SCOPE_VERSION = 'v-ext-events-ro';
57	
58	/**
59	 * A connection can CREATE calendar events (write) once it's been granted the
60	 * current `calendar.events` scope. Older read-only connections return false →
61	 * the Slack create-from-form flow prompts a lazy reconnect instead of writing.
62	 */
63	export function isWriteCapableConnection(conn: { scope_version?: string | null } | null | undefined): boolean {
64	  return conn?.scope_version === CURRENT_SCOPE_VERSION;
65	}
66	
67	/** True when the host has a live, write-capable connection (can create events via OAuth). */
68	export async function isHostWriteCapable(hostEmail: string): Promise<boolean> {
69	  const conn = await getCalendarConnection(hostEmail).catch(() => null);
70	  return !!conn && conn.status === 'connected' && !!conn.refresh_token && isWriteCapableConnection(conn);
71	}
72	
73	function stateSecret(): string {
74	  return process.env.VISITOR_ADMIN_SESSION_SECRET
75	    ?? process.env.VISITOR_ADMIN_SECRET
76	    ?? 'calendar-oauth-state-fallback';
77	}
78	
79	export function isCalendarOAuthConfigured(): boolean {
80	  return !!(googleOauthConfig.clientId() && googleOauthConfig.clientSecret() && googleOauthConfig.publicOrigin());
81	}
82	
83	export function isAuxilioExtensionOAuthConfigured(): boolean {
84	  return !!(auxilioExtensionOauth.clientId() && auxilioExtensionOauth.clientSecret());
85	}
86	
87	function callbackUrl(): string {
88	  const origin = (googleOauthConfig.publicOrigin() ?? '').replace(/\/$/, '');
89	  if (!origin) throw new Error('GOOGLE_OAUTH_PUBLIC_ORIGIN is not configured');
90	  return `${origin}/api/visitor/calendar/connect/callback`;
91	}
92	
93	function buildClient() {
94	  const clientId = googleOauthConfig.clientId();
95	  const clientSecret=[REDACTED].clientSecret();
96	  if (!clientId || !clientSecret) throw new Error('Google OAuth client credentials are not configured');
97	  return new google.auth.OAuth2(clientId, clientSecret, callbackUrl());
98	}
99	
100	// ─── Signed state (carries host binding, no server-side store) ────────
101	
102	export interface CalendarStatePayload {
103	  email: string;
104	  slackUserId?: string;
105	}
106	
107	export function issueCalendarState(payload: CalendarStatePayload): string {
108	  const body = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify({ ...payload, exp: Date.now() + STATE_TTL_MS })).toString('base64url');
109	  const sig = createHmac('sha256', stateSecret()).update(body).digest('base64url');
110	  return `${body}.${sig}`;
```

> AGENT

This is the key finding. `calendar-oauth.ts:32-35` documents the decision explicitly:

> *"Contacts were dropped: name resolution lives in the add-on (its own People API) + the invite forms; the poll no longer creates invites, so **backend contacts is dead**."*

So the add-on already resolves names **client-side** with its own People API call, and the engine's `calendar-contacts.ts` is confirmed-dead by design. That changes my Phase 2 approach for the better: rather than reviving a dead engine path and inventing a draft realtime channel, the extension should resolve names/photos **client-side in its background worker** — exactly mirroring the add-on, and the extension already calls Google APIs directly from the background with its own `access_token`.

**Course-correction (same UX, simpler + cheaper + matches the add-on):**
- No engine logic change, no draft-persistence churn, no realtime invention, no engine deploy for the feature.
- The only shared piece is the OAuth **scope** (consent), which I add to the extension's sign-in.
- The engine `nameIsFallback` flag I already added stays — it's how the panel knows which guests still need a real name.

```
1	/**
2	 * Typed RPC over chrome.runtime messaging. The side panel (and content script)
3	 * never touch tokens or the network directly — they send these messages and the
4	 * background worker does the work. This keeps the thin-adapter boundary: one
5	 * place holds secrets + network.
6	 */
7	import type {
8	  ActiveEvent,
9	  AuthStatus,
10	  DomEventSnapshot,
11	  DraftPatch,
12	  DraftResponse,
13	  PreviewResponse,
14	  SendResponse,
15	  VisitDraft,
16	  VisitorEventSummary,
17	} from './types';
18	
19	export type RpcRequest =
20	  | { type: 'AUTH_STATUS' }
21	  | { type: 'AUTH_SIGN_IN' }
22	  | { type: 'AUTH_SIGN_OUT' }
23	  | { type: 'OPEN_PANEL' }
24	  | { type: 'OPEN_FOR_EVENT'; eid: string; snapshot?: DomEventSnapshot }
25	  | { type: 'FOLLOW_EVENT'; eid: string; snapshot?: DomEventSnapshot }
26	  | { type: 'RESOLVE_EVENT'; eid: string }
27	  | { type: 'DRAFT_LOAD'; event: ActiveEvent }
28	  | { type: 'DRAFT_PATCH'; iCalUid: string; patch: DraftPatch }
29	  | { type: 'PREVIEW'; iCalUid: string; visitorEmail: string }
30	  | { type: 'SEND'; iCalUid: string; start?: string; end?: string }
31	  | { type: 'CANCEL_GUEST'; iCalUid: string; invitationId: string }
32	  | { type: 'LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS' }
33	  | { type: 'GET_NUDGE_TARGETS' }
34	  | { type: 'SYNC_NOW' }
35	  | { type: 'GET_PANEL_STATE' }
36	  | { type: 'NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT'; eventId?: string; eid?: string };
37	
38	/** Maps each request type to its success payload. */
39	export interface RpcResultMap {
40	  AUTH_STATUS: AuthStatus;
41	  AUTH_SIGN_IN: AuthStatus;
42	  AUTH_SIGN_OUT: { signedIn: false };
43	  OPEN_PANEL: { opened: boolean };
44	  OPEN_FOR_EVENT: { opened: boolean };
45	  FOLLOW_EVENT: { followed: boolean };
46	  RESOLVE_EVENT: ActiveEvent;
47	  DRAFT_LOAD: DraftResponse;
48	  DRAFT_PATCH: VisitDraft;
49	  PREVIEW: PreviewResponse;
50	  SEND: SendResponse;
51	  CANCEL_GUEST: { invitationId: string; cancelled: boolean };
52	  LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS: VisitorEventSummary[];
53	  GET_NUDGE_TARGETS: VisitorEventSummary[];
54	  SYNC_NOW: { synced: boolean };
55	  GET_PANEL_STATE: { open: boolean };
56	  NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT: { navigated: boolean };
57	}
58	
59	export type RpcResponse<T> =
60	  | { ok: true; data: T }
61	  | { ok: false; error: string; status?: number; needsAuth?: boolean };
62	
63	export class RpcError extends Error {
64	  constructor(
65	    message: string,
66	    readonly status?: number,
67	    readonly needsAuth?: boolean,
68	  ) {
69	    super(message);
70	    this.name = 'RpcError';
71	  }
72	}
73	
74	/** Side-panel-side caller: throws RpcError on failure, returns typed data. */
75	export async function rpc<K extends RpcRequest['type']>(
76	  msg: Extract<RpcRequest, { type: K }>,
77	): Promise<RpcResultMap[K]> {
78	  const res = (await chrome.runtime.sendMessage(msg)) as
79	    | RpcResponse<RpcResultMap[K]>
80	    | undefined;
81	  if (!res) throw new RpcError('No response from background worker');
82	  if (!res.ok) throw new RpcError(res.error, res.status, res.needsAuth);
83	  return res.data;
84	}
85	
86	/** storage.session key holding the pending event id (set by background on the
87	 *  content-script button click, read reactively by the side panel). */
88	export const ACTIVE_EID_KEY = 'auxilio.activeEid';
89	
90	/** storage.session key holding the DOM snapshot for the pending event — lets the
91	 *  panel paint instantly + handle the unsaved (no-API) case. */
92	export const ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY = 'auxilio.activeSnapshot';
93	
94	/** Background→content broadcast of side-panel open state, so the in-page button
95	 *  hides while the panel is open (no redundant control). */
96	export const PANEL_STATE = '__auxilio_panel_state';
97	
98	/** Port name the side panel connects on mount so background can track open/close. */
99	export const SIDEPANEL_PORT = 'auxilio.sidepanel';
100	
101	/** Background→content broadcast of the visitor events needing passes (drives the
102	 *  in-page nudge banner). */
103	export const NUDGE_TARGETS = '__auxilio_nudge_targets';
104	
105	/** Background→content broadcast: a recoverable auth lapse (the user was connected
106	 *  but a silent token renew failed). Drives the in-page "Reconnect Auxilio" banner
107	 *  so nudging never dies silently. `lapsed:false` clears it once renew succeeds. */
108	export const AUTH_LAPSED = '__auxilio_auth_lapsed';
109	
110	/** One-way background→panel broadcast: the active event changed on the server
111	 *  (post-save), so the panel should refetch. Not an RPC (no response). */
112	export const REFRESH_ACTIVE = '__auxilio_refresh_active';
113	
114	export function broadcastRefreshActive(): void {
115	  chrome.runtime.sendMessage({ type: REFRESH_ACTIVE }).catch(() => {});
116	}
```

```
26	const SYNC_ALARM = 'auxilio-sync';
27	const BADGE_COLOR = '#92288E';
28	// Cross-channel status poll throttle: don't ask the engine "which are handled"
29	// every alarm tick. Poll when the pending iCalUid set changes, else at most this
30	// often. Stored in storage.local so the throttle survives SW restarts.
31	const STATUS_POLL_KEY = 'auxilio.statusPoll';
32	const STATUS_POLL_INTERVAL_MS = 5 * 60_000;
33	// Muted/error badge for a recoverable auth lapse — visually distinct from the
34	// brand-tinted visitor-count badge so "nudging is offline" never reads as a count.
35	const LAPSED_BADGE_COLOR = '#B3261E';
36	
37	// Whether the side panel is currently open (a port is connected). Content
38	// scripts ask for this on (re)load so auto-follow works after navigation.
39	let panelConnected = false;
40	
41	export default defineBackground(() => {
42	  // Let content scripts read/write storage.session (default is trusted-only).
43	  // Needed for the active-event handoff + nudge-dismissal persistence.
44	  chrome.storage.session
45	    .setAccessLevel({ accessLevel: 'TRUSTED_AND_UNTRUSTED_CONTEXTS' })
46	    .catch((err) => console.warn('[auxilio] setAccessLevel failed', err));
47	
48	  // Toolbar icon opens the panel (Chrome's required user gesture).
49	  chrome.sidePanel
50	    .setPanelBehavior({ openPanelOnActionClick: true })
51	    .catch((err) => console.error('[auxilio] setPanelBehavior failed', err));
52	
53	  // Durable, official change-detection: poll on an alarm (no DOM).
54	  chrome.alarms.create(SYNC_ALARM, { periodInMinutes: 1 });
55	  chrome.runtime.onInstalled.addListener(() => {
56	    chrome.alarms.create(SYNC_ALARM, { periodInMinutes: 1 });
57	    // Re-scan from scratch on install/update: a config change (e.g. the magic
58	    // address) only takes effect on a full sync, since incremental never
59	    // re-reports an already-consumed change. clearSyncToken forces that.
60	    void clearSyncToken().then(() => doSync());
61	  });
62	  chrome.runtime.onStartup.addListener(() => void doSync());
63	  chrome.alarms.onAlarm.addListener((a) => {
64	    if (a.name === SYNC_ALARM) void doSync();
65	  });
66	  chrome.notifications.onClicked.addListener((id) => chrome.notifications.clear(id));
67	
68	  // Track the side panel open/closed via a port it connects on mount, and tell
69	  // calendar tabs so the in-page button can hide while the panel is open.
70	  chrome.runtime.onConnect.addListener((port) => {
71	    if (port.name !== SIDEPANEL_PORT) return;
72	    panelConnected = true;
73	    broadcastPanelState(true);
74	    port.onDisconnect.addListener(() => {
75	      panelConnected = false;
76	      broadcastPanelState(false);
77	    });
78	  });
79	
80	  chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener((msg: RpcRequest, sender, sendResponse) => {
81	    if (typeof msg?.type === 'string' && msg.type.startsWith('__')) return false; // broadcasts
82	    handle(msg, sender)
83	      .then(sendResponse)
84	      .catch((err) => sendResponse(fail(err)));
85	    return true; // async response
86	  });
87	
88	  console.log('[auxilio] background ready');
89	});

91	// ─────────────────────────── sync loop ───────────────────────────
92	
93	async function doSync(): Promise<void> {
94	  let tokens: { idToken: string; accessToken: string };
95	  try {
96	    tokens = await getValidTokens();
97	  } catch {
98	    // Token unavailable. If the user was connected, this is a recoverable lapse
99	    // (storage.session wiped on restart, or silent renew failed) — surface it so
100	    // nudging never dies silently. If they never connected, stay quiet.
101	    if (await wasConnected()) await setLapsed(true);
102	    return;
103	  }
104	  let result;
105	  try {
106	    result = await runSync(tokens.accessToken);
107	  } catch (err) {
108	    console.warn('[auxilio] sync failed', err);
109	    return;
110	  }
111	
112	  // Got here with working tokens → any prior lapse is over.
113	  await setLapsed(false);
114	
115	  // Cross-channel suppression: ask the engine which marked events already have an
116	  // active pass (issued via the add-on or another device), so we don't nag for
117	  // them. Gated/throttled so this isn't a per-minute engine call.
118	  const engineHandled = await maybePollEngineStatus(tokens.idToken);
119	
120	  // Toolbar badge = distinct visitor events still needing passes (engine-filtered,
121	  // series-collapsed) — sourced from listMarked so it matches the banner exactly.
122	  const targets = await listMarked();
123	  await chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: BADGE_COLOR });
124	  await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: targets.length > 0 ? String(targets.length) : '' });
125	
126	  // OS notification for each newly-discovered visitor event (out-of-tab alert) —
127	  // but never for one the engine has already handled elsewhere.
128	  for (const ev of result.newMarked) {
129	    if (!engineHandled.has((ev.iCalUid ?? '').toLowerCase())) notifyVisitorEvent(ev);
130	  }
131	
132	  // In-page nudge banner: push the current marked set to calendar tabs, plus
133	  // any events that just stopped being pending (deleted/cancelled) so the page
134	  // can purge stale optimistic nudges for them.
135	  broadcastNudge(targets, result.cancelledIds);
136	
137	  // If the event the panel is showing changed (post-save), tell it to refetch.
138	  const stored = await chrome.storage.session.get(ACTIVE_EID_KEY);
139	  const activeEid = stored[ACTIVE_EID_KEY] as string | undefined;
140	  if (activeEid) {
141	    const dec = decodeEid(activeEid);
142	    if (dec && result.changedIds.has(dec.eventId)) broadcastRefreshActive();
143	  }
144	}
145	
146	/** Ask the engine which pending events already have an active pass (handled via
147	 *  any surface), and cache the answer. Throttled: re-polls when the pending set
148	 *  changes, else at most every STATUS_POLL_INTERVAL_MS. Degrades gracefully — if
149	 *  the engine/route is unreachable it keeps the last known set (local-only
150	 *  suppression still works). Returns the (lower-cased) engine-handled set. */
151	async function maybePollEngineStatus(idToken: string): Promise<Set<string>> {
152	  const uids = await listPendingICalUids();
153	  if (uids.length === 0) {
154	    await setEngineHandled([]); // nothing pending → clear the cache
155	    return new Set();
156	  }
157	  const sig = [...uids].sort().join('|');
158	  const store = await chrome.storage.local.get(STATUS_POLL_KEY);
159	  const last = store[STATUS_POLL_KEY] as { at: number; sig: string } | undefined;
160	  const fresh = last && Date.now() - last.at < STATUS_POLL_INTERVAL_MS;
161	  if (last && last.sig === sig && fresh) return readEngineHandled(); // reuse cache
162	
163	  // Stamp the throttle now (before the call) so a missing/erroring route backs off
164	  // instead of retrying every tick. A changed signature still forces a re-poll.
165	  await chrome.storage.local.set({ [STATUS_POLL_KEY]: { at: Date.now(), sig } });
166	  try {
167	    const { active } = await engine.status(idToken, uids);
168	    await setEngineHandled(active);
169	    return new Set(active.map((u) => u.toLowerCase()));
170	  } catch (err) {
171	    console.warn('[auxilio] engine status poll failed; keeping last known', err);
172	    return readEngineHandled();
173	  }
174	}
175	
176	function broadcastPanelState(open: boolean): void {
177	  sendToCalendarTabs({ type: PANEL_STATE, open });
178	}
179	
180	function broadcastNudge(targets: VisitorEventSummary[], cancelled: string[] = []): void {
181	  sendToCalendarTabs({ type: NUDGE_TARGETS, targets, cancelled });
182	}
183	
184	/** Enter/leave the recoverable auth-lapsed state: a distinct toolbar badge plus a
185	 *  broadcast that drives the in-page "Reconnect" banner. Broadcast unconditionally
186	 *  so a calendar tab loaded mid-lapse learns about it on the next sync. */
187	async function setLapsed(lapsed: boolean): Promise<void> {
188	  if (lapsed) {
189	    await chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: LAPSED_BADGE_COLOR });
190	    await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: '!' });
191	  }
192	  sendToCalendarTabs({ type: AUTH_LAPSED, lapsed });
193	}
194	
195	/** Recompute the nudge surfaces from local state (no Calendar round-trip) — used
196	 *  after a send marks an event handled, so the badge/banner drop it immediately. */
197	async function refreshNudgeSurfaces(): Promise<void> {
198	  const targets = await listMarked();
199	  await chrome.action.setBadgeBackgroundColor({ color: BADGE_COLOR });
200	  await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: targets.length > 0 ? String(targets.length) : '' });
201	  broadcastNudge(targets);
202	}
203	
204	function sendToCalendarTabs(message: unknown): void {
205	  chrome.tabs
206	    .query({ url: 'https://calendar.google.com/*' })
207	    .then((tabs) => {
208	      for (const t of tabs) {
209	        if (t.id != null) chrome.tabs.sendMessage(t.id, message).catch(() => {});
210	      }
211	    })
212	    .catch(() => {});
213	}
214	
215	function notifyVisitorEvent(ev: VisitorEventSummary): void {
216	  chrome.notifications.create(`auxilio:${ev.eventId}`, {
217	    type: 'basic',
218	    iconUrl: chrome.runtime.getURL('icon/128.png'),
219	    title: 'Visitor event',
220	    message: `${ev.title}. Open Auxilio to register passes.`,
221	    priority: 1,
222	  });
223	}
224	
225	// ─────────────────────────── RPC plumbing ───────────────────────────
226	
227	const ok = <T,>(data: T): RpcResponse<T> => ({ ok: true, data });
228	
229	function fail(err: unknown): RpcResponse<never> {
230	  const message = err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err);
231	  const status = err instanceof EngineError ? err.status : undefined;
232	  return { ok: false, error: message, status, needsAuth: status === 401 };
233	}
234	
235	async function withTokens<T>(
236	  fn: (t: { idToken: string; accessToken: string; email?: string }) => Promise<T>,
237	): Promise<RpcResponse<T>> {
238	  let tokens;
239	  try {
240	    tokens = await getValidTokens();
241	  } catch {
242	    return { ok: false, error: 'Sign in to continue.', needsAuth: true };
243	  }
244	  try {
245	    return ok(await fn(tokens));
246	  } catch (err) {
247	    return fail(err);
248	  }
249	}
250	
251	const withIdToken = <T,>(fn: (idToken: string) => Promise<T>) =>
252	  withTokens((t) => fn(t.idToken));
253	
254	async function handle(
255	  msg: RpcRequest,
256	  sender: chrome.runtime.MessageSender,
257	): Promise<RpcResponse<unknown>> {
258	  switch (msg.type) {
259	    case 'AUTH_STATUS':
260	      return ok(await authStatus());
261	    case 'AUTH_SIGN_IN': {
262	      const status = await signIn();
263	      void doSync(); // warm the marked set + badge right after sign-in
264	      return ok(status);
265	    }
266	    case 'AUTH_SIGN_OUT':
267	      await signOut();
268	      await chrome.action.setBadgeText({ text: '' });
269	      return ok({ signedIn: false });
270	
271	    case 'OPEN_PANEL': {
272	      // Open the side panel within the in-page click's user activation (e.g. the
273	      // "Reconnect" banner) so the user lands on the sign-in gate. No active event.
274	      const tabId = sender.tab?.id;
275	      let opened = false;
276	      if (!panelConnected && tabId != null) {
277	        try {
278	          await chrome.sidePanel.open({ tabId });
279	          opened = true;
280	        } catch (err) {
281	          console.warn('[auxilio] sidePanel.open failed (gesture not carried?)', err);
282	        }
283	      }
284	      return ok({ opened });
285	    }
286	
287	    case 'OPEN_FOR_EVENT': {
288	      // The background is the single authority on open-vs-follow: panelConnected
289	      // (the panel's own port) is the truth, so the content script no longer
290	      // guesses with local flags that drift. Open the panel only when it isn't
291	      // already connected — otherwise this is just a "follow" and we fall through
292	      // to update the active event below. Call sidePanel.open FIRST, before any
293	      // await, so the content-script click's user activation is still valid
294	      // (sidePanel.open requires a live gesture).
295	      const tabId = sender.tab?.id;
296	      let opened = false;
297	      if (!panelConnected && tabId != null) {
298	        try {
299	          await chrome.sidePanel.open({ tabId });
300	          opened = true;
301	        } catch (err) {
302	          console.warn('[auxilio] sidePanel.open failed (gesture not carried?)', err);
303	        }
304	      }
305	      // Store after — the panel reacts to this via storage.onChanged either way.
306	      // The DOM snapshot lets the panel paint instantly + cover the unsaved case.
307	      await chrome.storage.session.set({
308	        [ACTIVE_EID_KEY]: msg.eid,
309	        [ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY]: msg.snapshot ?? null,
310	      });
311	      return ok({ opened });
312	    }
313	
314	    case 'FOLLOW_EVENT': {
315	      // Auto-follow: point the (already open) panel at the event the user is
316	      // viewing — no sidePanel.open. The panel applies its own busy-guard.
317	      await chrome.storage.session.set({
318	        [ACTIVE_EID_KEY]: msg.eid,
319	        [ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY]: msg.snapshot ?? null,
320	      });
321	      return ok({ followed: true });
322	    }
323	
324	    case 'RESOLVE_EVENT':
325	      return withTokens((t) => fetchActiveEvent(msg.eid, t.accessToken));
326	
327	    case 'DRAFT_LOAD':
328	      return withIdToken((t) => engine.loadDraft(t, msg.event));
329	    case 'DRAFT_PATCH':
330	      return withIdToken((t) => engine.patchDraft(t, msg.iCalUid, msg.patch));
331	    case 'PREVIEW':
332	      return withIdToken((t) => engine.preview(t, msg.iCalUid, msg.visitorEmail));
333	    case 'SEND':
334	      return withTokens(async (t) => {
335	        const result = await engine.send(t.idToken, msg.iCalUid, msg.start, msg.end);
336	        // Passes are now issued (or remain issued after an update) → drop this
337	        // event from the nudge surfaces. Prefer the event id we already hold
338	        // locally (the active eid — exactly the key the marked set uses); fall
339	        // back to the engine's echoed providerEventId. Not relying on the echo
340	        // closes a silent gap where a draft without providerEventId would never
341	        // get suppressed.
342	        if (result.activeCount > 0) {
343	          const stored = await chrome.storage.session.get(ACTIVE_EID_KEY);
344	          const eid = stored[ACTIVE_EID_KEY] as string | undefined;
345	          const eventId = (eid && decodeEid(eid)?.eventId) || result.draft.providerEventId;
346	          if (eventId) {
347	            await markHandled(eventId);
348	            await refreshNudgeSurfaces();
349	          }
350	        }
351	        return result;
352	      });
353	    case 'CANCEL_GUEST':
354	      return withIdToken((t) =>
355	        engine.cancelGuest(t, msg.iCalUid, msg.invitationId),
356	      );
357	
358	    case 'LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS':
359	      return withTokens(async (t) => {
360	        // A refresh hiccup (a transient Calendar API error) must not blank the
361	        // list — serve the last-known marked set instead of failing the query.
362	        try {
363	          await runSync(t.accessToken); // refresh before listing
364	        } catch (err) {
365	          console.warn('[auxilio] LIST_VISITOR_EVENTS refresh failed; serving cached set', err);
366	        }
367	        return listMarked();
368	      });
369	
370	    case 'GET_NUDGE_TARGETS':
371	      // Cheap read of the last-synced marked set (no token / no sync) — lets the
372	      // content script seed its banner immediately on page load.
373	      return ok(await listMarked());
374	
375	    case 'SYNC_NOW':
376	      // On-demand sync (page load / tab focus / just left the editor) so the
377	      // banner appears fast instead of waiting up to a minute for the alarm.
378	      await doSync();
379	      return ok({ synced: true });
380	
381	    case 'GET_PANEL_STATE':
382	      return ok({ open: panelConnected });
383	
384	    case 'NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT':
385	      return withTokens(async (t) => {
386	        // Open the event's full EDIT screen (/r/eventedit/<eid>) in the existing
387	        // calendar tab — same tab, no new tab. The button passes a ready eid
388	        // (from the page's data-eventid); the nudge passes eventId to encode
389	        // (sync events only carry the id; primary calendar id = the user's email).
390	        let editEid = msg.eid;
391	        if (!editEid && msg.eventId) {
392	          const email = t.email ?? (await authStatus()).email ?? 'primary';
393	          editEid = encodeEid(msg.eventId, email);
394	        }
395	        // The web editor needs the *real* calendar id in the eid — for the
396	        // primary calendar that's the user's email, not the literal "primary"
397	        // (which the Calendar API accepts but the /eventedit URL can't resolve).
398	        // Sync/nudge eids are encoded with "primary", so rewrite them here.
399	        if (editEid) {
400	          const dec = decodeEid(editEid);
401	          if (dec && (dec.calendarId === 'primary' || !dec.calendarId)) {
402	            const email = t.email ?? (await authStatus()).email;
403	            if (email) editEid = encodeEid(dec.eventId, email);
404	          }
405	        }
406	        // Google's eventedit eid is base64 with padding stripped — normalize the
407	        // raw data-eventid (which may carry `=` padding) to match.
408	        if (editEid) editEid = editEid.replace(/=+$/, '');
409	        if (!editEid) return { navigated: false };
410	        const tabs = await chrome.tabs.query({ url: 'https://calendar.google.com/*' });
411	        const tab = tabs.find((x) => x.active) ?? tabs[0];
412	        if (tab?.id == null || !tab.url) return { navigated: false };
413	        // Build the edit-screen URL from the /u/<n>/ account segment.
414	        const u = tab.url.match(/\/calendar\/(u\/\d+\/)?/);
415	        const userSeg = u?.[1] ?? '';
416	        const url = `https://calendar.google.com/calendar/${userSeg}r/eventedit/${editEid}`;
417	        console.log('[auxilio] navigate →', { from: tab.url, to: url, editEid });
418	        await chrome.tabs.update(tab.id, { url, active: true });
419	        return { navigated: true };
420	      });
421	
422	    default:
423	      return { ok: false, error: 'Unknown request' };
424	  }
425	}
426
```

```
29	import {
30	  useActiveEid,
31	  useActiveSnapshot,
32	  useAuthStatus,
33	  useDraft,
34	  usePatchDraft,
35	  usePreview,
36	  useResolveEvent,
37	  useSend,
38	  useVisitorEvents,
39	} from './hooks';
40	import { AccountMenu } from './AccountMenu';
41	import { EmptyState } from './EmptyState';
42	import { RosterRow } from './RosterRow';
43	import { PreviewSheet } from './PreviewSheet';
44	import { SignInGate } from './SignInGate';
45	
46	const isAuthError = (err: unknown) => err instanceof RpcError && !!err.needsAuth;
47	
48	export function VisitPanel() {
49	  // `storedEid` is what the content script is pointing us at (auto-follow). We
50	  // freeze the *displayed* `eid` while busy so following never switches the view
51	  // mid-action (Guard 1); edits already persist on blur (Guard 2).
52	  const storedEid = useActiveEid();
53	  const snapshot = useActiveSnapshot();
54	  const [eid, setEid] = useState<string | null>(storedEid);
55	  const auth = useAuthStatus();
56	  // Picker fuels the "open from list" empty state (and graceful fallback).
57	  const visitorEvents = useVisitorEvents(!eid && !!auth.data?.signedIn);
58	  const resolve = useResolveEvent(eid);
59	  const event = resolve.data;
60	  const draft = useDraft(event);
61	
62	  const patch = usePatchDraft(event?.iCalUid);
63	  const send = useSend(event?.iCalUid, event?.start, event?.end);
64	  const preview = usePreview(event?.iCalUid);
65	
66	  const [previewData, setPreviewData] = useState<{
67	    preview: PreviewResponse;
68	    recipient: string;
69	  } | null>(null);
70	  // Tracks host edits the roster can't otherwise reveal — a name/phone change or
71	  // template switch on an already-issued event leaves no structural delta, so we
72	  // remember "the host touched something" to keep the update button live. Reset
73	  // when the event changes or a send succeeds (the draft then reflects reality).
74	  const [edited, setEdited] = useState(false);
75	  useEffect(() => setEdited(false), [event?.iCalUid]);
76	
77	  // Guard 1: hold the current event while sending / previewing; catch up once idle.
78	  const busy = send.isPending || preview.isPending || !!previewData;
79	  useEffect(() => {
80	    if (!busy) setEid(storedEid);
81	  }, [storedEid, busy]);
82	
83	  // Post-save refresh: the background sync poll broadcasts when the active event
84	  // changed on the server (no DOM); refetch so the roster reflects saved state.
85	  const qc = useQueryClient();
86	  useEffect(() => {
87	    const listener = (msg: { type?: string }) => {
88	      if (msg?.type === REFRESH_ACTIVE) {
89	        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['resolve'] });
90	        qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['draft'] });
91	      }
92	    };
93	    chrome.runtime.onMessage.addListener(listener);
94	    return () => chrome.runtime.onMessage.removeListener(listener);
95	  }, [qc]);
96	
97	  // Back → clear the active event/snapshot, returning to the home list.
98	  const clearActive = () =>
99	    chrome.storage.session.remove([ACTIVE_EID_KEY, ACTIVE_SNAPSHOT_KEY]);
100	  // Show Back in every screen except the pure home (no event, no snapshot).
101	  const back = eid || snapshot?.magicPresent ? clearActive : undefined;
102	
103	  // ---- gating states ----
104	  if (!eid) {
105	    // New, not-yet-saved visitor event (no eid, but the DOM saw the magic
106	    // address) → preview the detected guests + prompt to save.
107	    if (snapshot?.magicPresent) {
108	      return (
109	        <Shell onBack={back}>
110	          <NoticeState
111	            title="Save the event to issue passes"
112	            body="This event isn’t saved yet, so passes can’t be issued. Add your guests and save it in Google Calendar, then reopen Manage Visitors."
113	            guests={snapshot.guestEmails}
114	          />
115	        </Shell>
116	      );
117	    }
118	    return (
119	      <Shell>
120	        <EmptyState
121	          auth={auth.data}
122	          events={visitorEvents.data}
123	          loading={visitorEvents.isLoading}
124	          onPick={(ev) => {
125	            // Just point the panel at the picked event — no tab navigation. Use
126	            // "Open in Calendar" in the summary to jump to it explicitly.
127	            chrome.storage.session.set({ [ACTIVE_EID_KEY]: ev.eid });
128	          }}
129	        />
130	      </Shell>
131	    );
132	  }
133	  if (isAuthError(resolve.error) || isAuthError(draft.error)) {
134	    return <Shell onBack={back}><SignInGate /></Shell>;
135	  }
136	  if (resolve.isLoading) return <Shell onBack={back}><LoadingState label="Reading event…" /></Shell>;
137	  if (resolve.isError) {
138	    const msg = (resolve.error as Error).message;
139	    if (msg === 'NOT_SAVED') {
140	      return (
141	        <Shell onBack={back}>
142	          <NoticeState
143	            title="Save the event to issue passes"
144	            body="This event isn’t saved yet, so passes can’t be issued. Save it in Google Calendar, then reopen Manage Visitors."
145	            onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()}
146	            guests={snapshot?.guestEmails}
147	          />
148	        </Shell>
149	      );
150	    }
151	    return (
152	      <Shell onBack={back}>
153	        <ErrorState message={msg} onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()} />
154	      </Shell>
155	    );
156	  }
157	  // Event resolved but no iCalUID → the draft query can't run (would sit on a
158	  // skeleton forever). Surface it instead of hanging.
159	  if (event && !event.iCalUid) {
160	    return (
161	      <Shell onBack={back}>
162	        <NoticeState
163	          title="Can’t register from this event"
164	          body="This event has no calendar UID yet. This usually means it is not fully saved. Save it in Google Calendar and try again."
165	          onRetry={() => resolve.refetch()}
166	        />
167	      </Shell>
168	    );
169	  }
170	  if (draft.isError) {
171	    return (
172	      <Shell onBack={back}>
173	        <ErrorState message={(draft.error as Error).message} onRetry={() => draft.refetch()} />
174	      </Shell>
175	    );
176	  }
177	  if (!draft.data) {
178	    return <Shell onBack={back}><RosterSkeleton title={event?.title} /></Shell>;
179	  }
180	
181	  const data = draft.data;
182	  const included = data.roster.filter((g) => g.include);
183	  const sentCount = data.roster.filter((g) => g.status === 'sent').length;
184	
185	  // What's actually pending. A new/uninvited guest still to get a pass, or a sent
186	  // guest toggled off (a pending revoke), is a structural change visible in the
187	  // roster. Name/phone/template edits aren't — `edited` covers those.
188	  const pendingNew = data.roster.filter(
189	    (g) => g.include && g.status !== 'sent' && g.status !== 'cancelled',
190	  );
191	  const pendingCancel = data.roster.filter((g) => g.status === 'sent' && !g.include);
192	  const hasChanges = pendingNew.length > 0 || pendingCancel.length > 0 || edited;
193	  // Already-issued event with literally nothing to do → no point re-sending.
194	  const nothingToDo = data.materialized && !hasChanges;
195	
196	  const templates = data.emailTemplates;
197	  // One template for the whole event (engine stores per-guest; we set all guests
198	  // to the chosen key).
199	  const currentTemplate =
200	    data.roster.find((g) => g.emailTemplateKey)?.emailTemplateKey ??
201	    templates.find((t) => t.isDefault)?.key ??
202	    templates[0]?.key;
203	
204	  const patchGuest = (email: string, edit: Record<string, unknown>) => {
205	    setEdited(true);
206	    patch.mutate({ guests: [{ email, ...edit }] });
207	  };
208	
209	  const setEventTemplate = (key: string) => {
210	    setEdited(true);
211	    patch.mutate({ guests: data.roster.map((g) => ({ email: g.email, emailTemplateKey: key })) });
212	  };
213	
214	  // Review & send: render the real email for a representative included guest,
215	  // then Send/Cancel from the preview.
216	  const reviewEmail = included[0]?.email;
217	  const openReview = () => {
218	    if (!reviewEmail) return;
219	    preview.mutate(reviewEmail, {
220	      onSuccess: (d) => setPreviewData({ preview: d, recipient: reviewEmail }),
221	    });
222	  };
223	
224	  // First send needs at least one included guest; an already-issued event needs a
225	  // real pending change — otherwise the button is irrelevant and stays disabled.
226	  const canSend =
227	    !send.isPending && (data.materialized ? hasChanges : included.length > 0);
228	  const reviewLabel = data.materialized ? 'Review & update passes' : 'Review & send passes';
229	
230	  return (
231	    <Shell onBack={back}>
232	      <div className="enter" style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
233	        {/* Event summary */}
234	        <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-sm)' }}>
235	          <div className="type-title-lg">{event?.title || 'Untitled event'}</div>
236	          {event?.start && (
237	            <div className="type-body text-muted" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
238	              <Clock size={15} strokeWidth={2} />
239	              {formatWhen(event.start, event.end)}
240	            </div>
241	          )}
242	          {event?.rooms && event.rooms.length > 0 && (
243	            <div className="type-body text-muted" style={{ display: 'flex', alignItems: 'center', gap: 6 }}>
244	              <DoorOpen size={15} strokeWidth={2} />
245	              <span className="row__ellipsis">{event.rooms.join(', ')}</span>
246	            </div>
247	          )}
248	          {event?.description && (
249	            <div
250	              className="type-label-sm text-muted"
251	              style={{
252	                whiteSpace: 'pre-wrap',
253	                maxHeight: 72,
254	                overflow: 'hidden',
255	                borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
256	                paddingTop: 'var(--space-sm)',
257	              }}
258	            >
259	              {event.description}
260	            </div>
261	          )}
262	          {eid && (
263	            <Button
264	              variant="text"
265	              icon={<ExternalLink size={16} strokeWidth={2} />}
266	              onClick={() => void rpc({ type: 'NAVIGATE_TO_EVENT', eid })}
267	              // Negative left margin = the text button's own horizontal padding,
268	              // so the label optically aligns to the card edge while the hit area
269	              // and hover state layer stay intact (MD3 text-button placement).
270	              style={{ justifySelf: 'start', marginLeft: 'calc(-1 * var(--space-md))' }}
271	            >
272	              Open in Calendar
273	            </Button>
274	          )}
275	          {templates.length >= 2 && (
276	            <SelectField
277	              label="Email template (all guests)"
278	              value={currentTemplate}
279	              onChange={(e) => setEventTemplate(e.target.value)}
280	            >
281	              {templates.map((t) => (
282	                <option key={t.key} value={t.key}>
283	                  {t.name}
284	                  {t.isDefault ? ' (default)' : ''}
285	                </option>
286	              ))}
287	            </SelectField>
288	          )}
289	        </Card>
290	
291	        {/* Roster */}
292	        <section>
293	          <div
294	            className="type-label-sm text-muted"
295	            style={{ textTransform: 'uppercase', marginBottom: 4 }}
296	          >
297	            Guests · {included.length} getting a pass
298	          </div>
299	          {data.roster.length === 0 ? (
300	            <Card className="type-body text-muted">
301	              No guests on this event yet. Add attendees in Calendar, then reopen.
302	            </Card>
303	          ) : (
304	            <div className="roster">
305	              {data.roster.map((g) => (
306	                <RosterRow
307	                  key={g.email}
308	                  guest={g}
309	                  onChange={(edit) => patchGuest(g.email, edit)}
310	                />
311	              ))}
312	            </div>
313	          )}
314	        </section>
315	
316	        {/* Send result */}
317	        {send.isSuccess && <SendSummary result={send.data} />}
318	        {send.isError && (
319	          <div className="banner banner--error" role="alert">
320	            <AlertCircle size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
321	            <span className="type-body">{(send.error as Error).message}</span>
322	          </div>
323	        )}
324	      </div>
325	
326	      {/* Sticky action bar */}
327	      <footer
328	        style={{
329	          padding: 'var(--space-md) var(--space-lg)',
330	          borderTop: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
331	          background: 'var(--color-surface-lowest)',
332	        }}
333	      >
334	        {nothingToDo && (
335	          <div
336	            className="banner banner--info"
337	            role="status"
338	            style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--space-sm)' }}
339	          >
340	            <CheckCircle2 size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
341	            <span className="type-body">Up to date. No changes to send.</span>
342	          </div>
343	        )}
344	        <Button
345	          block
346	          loading={preview.isPending}
347	          disabled={!canSend}
348	          icon={<Eye size={18} strokeWidth={2} />}
349	          onClick={openReview}
350	        >
351	          {preview.isPending
352	            ? 'Loading preview…'
353	            : `${reviewLabel}${included.length ? ` (${included.length})` : ''}`}
354	        </Button>
355	        {preview.isError && (
356	          <div className="type-label-sm" style={{ color: 'var(--color-error)', textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6 }}>
357	            {(preview.error as Error).message}
358	          </div>
359	        )}
360	        {sentCount > 0 && !send.isPending && !nothingToDo && (
361	          <div className="type-label-sm text-muted" style={{ textAlign: 'center', marginTop: 6 }}>
362	            {sentCount} pass{sentCount > 1 ? 'es' : ''} already issued
363	          </div>
364	        )}
365	      </footer>
366	
367	      {previewData && (
368	        <PreviewSheet
369	          preview={previewData.preview}
370	          recipient={previewData.recipient}
371	          totalCount={included.length}
372	          sending={send.isPending}
373	          onSend={() =>
374	            send.mutate(undefined, {
375	              onSuccess: () => {
376	                setPreviewData(null);
377	                setEdited(false);
378	              },
379	            })
380	          }
381	          onClose={() => setPreviewData(null)}
382	        />
383	      )}
384	    </Shell>
385	  );
386	}
387	
388	// ───────────────────────── sub-components ─────────────────────────
389	
390	function Shell({ children, onBack }: { children: React.ReactNode; onBack?: () => void }) {
391	  return (
392	    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', height: '100%', minWidth: 320 }}>
393	      <header
394	        style={{
395	          display: 'flex',
396	          alignItems: 'center',
397	          gap: 'var(--space-sm)',
398	          padding: 'var(--space-md) var(--space-lg)',
399	          borderBottom: '1px solid var(--color-outline-variant)',
400	          flex: '0 0 auto',
401	        }}
402	      >
403	        {onBack ? (
404	          <IconButton label="Back to events" onClick={onBack}>
405	            <ArrowLeft size={20} strokeWidth={2} />
406	          </IconButton>
407	        ) : (
408	          <Logo size={26} />
409	        )}
410	        <span className="type-title">Auxilio Visitor</span>
411	        <span style={{ flex: 1 }} />
412	        <AccountMenu />
413	      </header>
414	      <div style={{ flex: 1, minHeight: 0, display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', overflowY: 'auto' }}>
415	        {children}
416	      </div>
417	    </div>
418	  );
419	}
420	
421	function LoadingState({ label }: { label: string }) {
422	  return (
423	    <div
424	      style={{
425	        flex: 1,
426	        display: 'flex',
427	        flexDirection: 'column',
428	        alignItems: 'center',
429	        justifyContent: 'center',
430	        gap: 'var(--space-md)',
431	        color: 'var(--color-on-surface-variant)',
432	      }}
433	    >
434	      <span className="spinner" style={{ width: 22, height: 22 }} />
435	      <span className="type-body">{label}</span>
436	    </div>
437	  );
438	}
439	
440	function RosterSkeleton({ title }: { title?: string }) {
441	  return (
442	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
443	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 8 }}>
444	        <span className="type-title-lg">{title || 'Loading event…'}</span>
445	        <Skeleton width="60%" />
446	      </Card>
447	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
448	        {[0, 1, 2].map((i) => (
449	          <div key={i} style={{ display: 'flex', gap: 'var(--space-md)', alignItems: 'center' }}>
450	            <Skeleton width={44} height={24} radius={999} />
451	            <div style={{ flex: 1, display: 'grid', gap: 6 }}>
452	              <Skeleton width="50%" />
453	              <Skeleton width="70%" height={12} />
454	            </div>
455	          </div>
456	        ))}
457	      </Card>
458	    </div>
459	  );
460	}
461	
462	function NoticeState({
463	  title,
464	  body,
465	  onRetry,
466	  guests,
467	}: {
468	  title: string;
469	  body: string;
470	  onRetry?: () => void;
471	  guests?: string[];
472	}) {
473	  return (
474	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)', display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-md)' }}>
475	      <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-sm)', padding: 'var(--space-xl)' }}>
476	        <span className="type-title-lg">{title}</span>
477	        <span className="type-body text-muted">{body}</span>
478	        {onRetry && (
479	          <Button
480	            variant="tonal"
481	            icon={<RefreshCw size={16} strokeWidth={2} />}
482	            onClick={onRetry}
483	            style={{ justifySelf: 'start', marginTop: 'var(--space-xs)' }}
484	          >
485	            Check again
486	          </Button>
487	        )}
488	      </Card>
489	      {guests && guests.length > 0 && (
490	        <Card style={{ display: 'grid', gap: 'var(--space-xs)' }}>
491	          <span className="type-label-sm text-muted" style={{ textTransform: 'uppercase' }}>
492	            Guests detected on this event
493	          </span>
494	          {guests.map((g) => (
495	            <span key={g} className="type-body row__ellipsis">{g}</span>
496	          ))}
497	        </Card>
498	      )}
499	    </div>
500	  );
501	}
502	
503	function ErrorState({ message, onRetry }: { message: string; onRetry: () => void }) {
504	  return (
505	    <div style={{ padding: 'var(--space-lg)' }}>
506	      <div className="banner banner--error" role="alert" style={{ marginBottom: 'var(--space-md)' }}>
507	        <AlertCircle size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
508	        <span className="type-body">{message}</span>
509	      </div>
510	      <Button variant="tonal" icon={<RefreshCw size={16} strokeWidth={2} />} onClick={onRetry}>
511	        Try again
512	      </Button>
513	    </div>
514	  );
515	}
516	
517	function SendSummary({
518	  result,
519	}: {
520	  result: import('@/lib/types').SendResponse;
521	}) {
522	  return (
523	    <div
524	      className="banner"
525	      role="status"
526	      aria-live="polite"
527	      style={{ background: 'var(--color-success-container)', color: 'var(--color-on-success-container)' }}
528	    >
529	      <CheckCircle2 size={18} strokeWidth={2} style={{ flex: '0 0 auto' }} />
530	      <div>
531	        <div className="type-label">
532	          {result.created.length > 0
533	            ? `${result.created.length} pass${result.created.length > 1 ? 'es' : ''} sent`
534	            : result.cancelled.length > 0
535	              ? 'Passes updated'
536	              : 'No changes'}
537	          {result.cancelled.length > 0 && ` · ${result.cancelled.length} cancelled`}
538	        </div>
539	        {result.failed.length > 0 && (
540	          <ul className="type-label-sm" style={{ margin: '4px 0 0', paddingLeft: 16 }}>
541	            {result.failed.map((f) => (
542	              <li key={f.visitorEmail}>
543	                {f.visitorEmail}: {f.reason}
544	              </li>
545	            ))}
546	          </ul>
547	        )}
548	      </div>
549	    </div>
550	  );
551	}
552	
553	function formatWhen(start: string, end?: string): string {
554	  const allDay = !start.includes('T');
555	  const s = new Date(start);
556	  const date = s.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' });
557	  if (allDay) return `${date} · All day`;
558	  const t = (d: Date) => d.toLocaleTimeString(undefined, { hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' });
559	  return end ? `${date} · ${t(s)} to ${t(new Date(end))}` : `${date} · ${t(s)}`;
560	}
561
```

```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/appointy/work/OfficeOperationsUmbrella/auxilio-extension/lib/people.ts",
  "content": "/**\n * People API name + photo resolution (background only).\n *\n * Why: Google omits `attendee.displayName` for external non-contact guests — the\n * name Calendar's web UI shows is resolved client-side from the host's Google\n * Contacts and never reaches the event API. We replicate that with the host's own\n * `access_token` (the same client-side pattern the Calendar add-on uses), so the\n * side panel can upgrade a guest's email-derived fallback name (and show a profile\n * photo) with no backend round-trip.\n *\n * Needs contacts.readonly + contacts.other.readonly on the OAuth consent (see\n * lib/auth SCOPES). Resolves only emails the host has saved or interacted with —\n * fails gracefully (returns nothing) for everyone else. Never throws.\n */\nimport type { ResolvedPerson } from './types';\n\nconst PEOPLE_BASE = 'https://people.googleapis.com/v1';\nconst CACHE_KEY = 'auxilio.peopleCache';\nconst CACHE_TTL_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // names/photos change rarely\nconst CACHE_MAX = 1000;\n\ntype CacheEntry = { person: ResolvedPerson | null; at: number };\n\n// In-memory cache for this service-worker's life (null = looked up, not found).\n// MV3 workers are short-lived, so we also persist to storage.local below.\nconst mem = new Map<string, ResolvedPerson | null>();\nlet warmed = false;\n\ninterface PeoplePerson {\n  names?: { displayName?: string }[];\n  emailAddresses?: { value?: string }[];\n  photos?: { url?: string; default?: boolean }[];\n}\n\nasync function search(\n  path: string,\n  query: string,\n  readMask: string,\n  accessToken: string,\n): Promise<{ results?: { person?: PeoplePerson }[] }> {\n  const url = new URL(`${PEOPLE_BASE}/${path}`);\n  url.searchParams.set('query', query);\n  url.searchParams.set('readMask', readMask);\n  url.searchParams.set('pageSize', '5');\n  const res = await fetch(url, { headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${accessToken}` } });\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`People ${path} ${res.status}`);\n  return res.json();\n}\n\n/** Google primes the search index only after a prior request, so an empty-query\n *  call must precede the first real one. Once per worker life. */\nasync function warmup(accessToken: string): Promise<void> {\n  if (warmed) return;\n  warmed = true;\n  await Promise.all([\n    search('people:searchContacts', '', 'names,emailAddresses', accessToken).catch(() => undefined),\n    search('otherContacts:search', '', 'names,emailAddresses', accessToken).catch(() => undefined),\n  ]);\n}\n\n/** A result only counts if it actually carries the email we searched (the search is\n *  fuzzy). Skip Google's generic silhouette photo (`default: true`). */\nfunction pick(person: PeoplePerson | undefined, email: string): ResolvedPerson | undefined {\n  if (!person) return undefined;\n  const emails = person.emailAddresses ?? [];\n  if (!emails.some((e) => e.value?.toLowerCase() === email.toLowerCase())) return undefined;\n  const name = person.names?.[0]?.displayName?.trim();\n  const photo = (person.photos ?? []).find((p) => p.url && !p.default);\n  const out: ResolvedPerson = {};\n  if (name) out.name = name;\n  if (photo?.url) out.photoUrl = photo.url;\n  return out.name || out.photoUrl ? out : undefined;\n}\n\nasync function resolveOne(email: string, accessToken: string): Promise<ResolvedPerson | null> {\n  try {\n    const own = await search('people:searchContacts', email, 'names,emailAddresses,photos', accessToken);\n    for (const r of own.results ?? []) {\n      const hit = pick(r.person, email);\n      if (hit) return hit;\n    }\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.warn('[auxilio] searchContacts failed', email, err);\n  }\n  try {\n    // otherContacts readMask does not reliably support photos → names only.\n    const other = await search('otherContacts:search', email, 'names,emailAddresses', accessToken);\n    for (const r of other.results ?? []) {\n      const hit = pick(r.person, email);\n      if (hit) return hit;\n    }\n  } catch (err) {\n    console.warn('[auxilio] otherContacts.search failed', email, err);\n  }\n  return null;\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve real name + photo for the given guest emails. Returns only the ones we\n * found something for. Cached (memory + storage.local with a TTL), looked up in\n * parallel, and best-effort — a failure for one email never rejects the whole call.\n */\nexport async function resolveGuests(\n  accessToken: string,\n  emails: string[],\n): Promise<Record<string, ResolvedPerson>> {\n  const want = [...new Set(emails.map((e) => e.toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean))];\n  if (want.length === 0) return {};\n\n  const persisted = await loadCache();\n  const out: Record<string, ResolvedPerson> = {};\n  const todo: string[] = [];\n\n  for (const email of want) {\n    const m = mem.get(email);\n    if (m !== undefined) {\n      if (m) out[email] = m;\n      continue;\n    }\n    const p = persisted[email];\n    if (p && Date.now() - p.at < CACHE_TTL_MS) {\n      mem.set(email, p.person);\n      if (p.person) out[email] = p.person;\n      continue;\n    }\n    todo.push(email);\n  }\n\n  if (todo.length > 0) {\n    await warmup(accessToken);\n    const results = await Promise.all(todo.map((e) => resolveOne(e, accessToken)));\n    todo.forEach((email, i) => {\n      const person = results[i];\n      mem.set(email, person);\n      persisted[email] = { person, at: Date.now() };\n      if (person) out[email] = person;\n    });\n    await saveCache(persisted);\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\nasync function loadCache(): Promise<Record<string, CacheEntry>> {\n  try {\n    const r = await chrome.storage.local.get(CACHE_KEY);\n    return (r[CACHE_KEY] as Record<string, CacheEntry>) ?? {};\n  } catch {\n    return {};\n  }\n}\n\nasync function saveCache(cache: Record<string, CacheEntry>): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    // Drop expired entries, then cap to the most recent CACHE_MAX so the store\n    // can't grow without bound.\n    const now = Date.now();\n    let entries = Object.entries(cache).filter(([, v]) => now - v.at < CACHE_TTL_MS);\n    if (entries.length > CACHE_MAX) {\n      entries = entries.sort((a, b) => b[1].at - a[1].at).slice(0, CACHE_MAX);\n    }\n    await chrome.storage.local.set({ [CACHE_KEY]: Object.fromEntries(entries) });\n  } catch {\n    /* best-effort cache */\n  }\n}\n"
}
```
